### BoK Gate check (v3 — MCORCH Master Execution Protocol)

For each BoK suite in `docs/bok/<slug>/`, verify completeness:
```bash
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete" || echo "⚠️ $slug — missing: ${missing[*]}"
done
```

If the user mentions working on a **new module / complex feature / integration / architectural change**:
- Check whether `docs/bok/<expected-slug>/` exists with all 9 docs.
- If missing: surface in the **ALERTAS** section as a critical block per CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol.
- Recommend `/bok-scribe <idea summary>` before any code is written.

## Rules

- **TypeScript errors → report immediately.** If there are TS errors, list them under ALERTAS. Do not start implementing new features until errors are resolved (unless the user explicitly asks to proceed despite errors).
- **Unhealthy containers → report with triage.** Suggest the fix command if known (e.g., `docker compose restart mcorch-vector-engine` for chroma).
- **Uncommitted changes → describe, do not commit.** Only describe what exists in the working tree; committing is the user's call.
- **Keep the brief tight.** No padding. No repetition of CLAUDE.md architecture. The user already knows the architecture — they need the delta: what changed, what's broken, what's next.
- **Pending Actions priority order:** CRITICAL (before first client) > NON-CRITICAL > Phase Zeta > Core v3 > Epsilon v9.
- **Knowledge Mesh numbers:** use `GraphRAG State` section from HANDOFF.md as the source. Do not invent counts.

> ## Canvas Studio Phase 4.2a — Typed Nodes & Inspectors Record (2026-05-15)
> 
> Sessão de continuidade do v6.2.0 — janela com 72% disponível após o seal OpenClaw + Higgsfield Validation. Sovereign autorizou avanço para Phase 4.2a (typed nodes + rich inspectors port from viralmind-builder) antes do /handoff de fim de sessão. Cinco sub-fases entregues em sequência, todas validadas com `npx tsc --noEmit` zero erros + `bun run test` 122/122 passa + `bun run build` 21s.
> 
> **1) Foundation (types + catálogos):** `src/types/canvas.ts` reescrito de 101 LOC permissivos para 622 LOC tipados. Discriminated union `CanvasNodeKind` (generateImage · characterReference · sceneCompose · styleTransfer · imageToVideo) com type-guards exportados. MODEL_INFO populado com pricing canônico do Higgsfield (Soul 720p×1=20mco/1.5hf, 720p×4=26mco/2hf, 1080p×1=40mco/3hf, 1080p×4=65mco/5hf) + DALL·E 3/GPT Image 1/FLUX 1.1 Pro/FLUX Schnell/SDXL/OpenRouter. VIDEO_MODEL_INFO com DoP Lite/Turbo/Standard + Kling 2.1 Pro + Seedance v1 (5 modelos). `resolveExecutePayload()` traduz shape tipado → request body do canvas-execute, garantindo que o helper `resolveHiggsfieldSoulKey` compute o tier preciso.
> 
> **2) Button accent + 5 typed node components:** variant `accent` no shadcn Button. Cinco componentes em `src/components/canvas/nodes/` cada um discriminando por kind e renderizando UI específica — preview adequado (quadrado, video, retangular), badge de status, custo dual (mcoCoins + hf credits quando aplicável), Handle target/source canônicos.
> 
> **3) Recipes rebuild:** `src/lib/canvas-recipes.ts` reescrito (343→416 LOC). Pipeline recipes (Story Arc / Mood Board / Reels Viral) mapeiam Col 0–2 → GenerateImage e Col 3 → ImageToVideo. Quick recipes em cadeia linear de GenerateImage com aspect ratio derivado da intenção (9:16 default, 16:9 banners/thumbnails, 4:5 feed beauty). Lane specs preservados.
> 
> **4) RightPanel + 5 inspectors + StyleSettingsPanel:** `src/components/canvas/RightPanel/` (8 arquivos, ~1100 LOC totais). shared.tsx exporta primitivos (Section/CostPreview/InspectorHeader/InheritanceBadge/ApplyInheritanceButton) + hook `useUpstreamCharacterCandidate` para smart inheritance via edges. StyleSettingsPanel renderiza quando nada selecionado (paleta + mood + style preset). Cinco inspectors:
> 
> - **GenerateImage:** modelo agrupado por provider (Higgsfield Soul 4 tiers · OpenAI · Replicate · OpenRouter), prompt 1000 chars, negative collapsible, AR canônico, 5 controls com tooltips info, seed manual+random, CostPreview dual.
> - **CharacterReference:** upload `canvas-assets` com signed URL 7d, nome do personagem, Soul Character disabled (Phase 4.5).
> - **SceneCompose:** prompt da cena + 4 Soul tiers + AR + smart inheritance badge/button.
> - **StyleTransfer:** 8 presets visuais aplicados via prompt engineering (suffix concatenado ao upstream prompt).
> - **ImageToVideo:** UI completa navegável, execução **disabled** com aviso Phase 4.2b explícito.
> 
> Adaptações do port: `useServerFn → useCanvasExecute`, `useMe → creditsBalance prop`, bucket `canvas-outputs → canvas-assets`, tokens `bg-elevated → bg-muted`, `text-warning → text-amber-500`.
> 
> **5) AddNodeMenu + registry:** `canvas-node-registry.ts` (5 entries com aliases pt-BR + factory pointer) + `AddNodeMenu.tsx` exportando `AddNodeFab` (Popover bottom-right + Input de busca + lista categorizada Inputs/Generate/Transform) e `AddNodeCommand` (Cmd+K palette via cmdk + value composto label+aliases+description para fuzzy match).
> 
> **6) CanvasEditorPage refactor:** -365 LOC, +84 LOC. Remove CanvasNodeCard inline + NODE_TYPES_CONFIG + Sheet overlay + estado local de prompt/provider/model. Adiciona 5 typed nodeTypes registrations + RightPanel aside fixo + AddNodeFab + AddNodeCommand. Run-All BFS reescrito sobre `resolveExecutePayload()` — pula characterReference (upload-only) e imageToVideo (Phase 4.2b deferido). Custo agregado via `estimateNodeCost()` que lê MODEL_INFO/VIDEO_MODEL_INFO conforme o kind.

## PHASE 2: AGENT ARMY SCALABILITY (THE PANTHEON ARCHITECTURE)

We are transitioning from a squad of 5 to a **Departmental Army**. Adjust `~/.openclaw/agents/main` and the root config to support this hierarchy:

* **Departmental Heads (L1):** * `Head of Engineering` (Owner of SWEBOK/Infra/Self-healing).

* Automate the `agents add` protocol for new recruits to ensure they immediately point to the canonical `/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra` workspace.



## PHASE 3: AUTONOMIC "NIGHTLY DREAMING" ALIGNMENT

Configure the native OpenClaw Cron to execute the **Conceptual Fine-tuning** every day at 03:00 AM:

* **Task:** The Guardian parses the daily deltas (commits/logs) + new Memory Ingestion.
* **Self-Optimization:** Update `CLAUDE.md` and agent instructions based on the "Nightly Dream" insights.

# EXECUTIVE DIRECTIVE: OPENCLAW V6.3.1 — NEURAL SYNCHRONIZATION & AGENT ARMY ARCHITECTURE

> **Context:** Sprint v6.3.0 is SHIPPED (Canvas 4.2a). The environment is live on Oracle Cloud (panel.gcrux.com).
> **Status:** OpenClaw Bootstrap is done, but we have a **Zero-Memory Crisis**. `memory status` reports 0 indexed chunks and missing directories, while the **Knowledge Mesh** has **8,236 nodes** in Supabase/Chroma.
> **Objective:** Bridge the memory gap and architect a scalable "Army of Agents" (Department Heads + Sub-agents).

---

## PHASE 1: THE NEURAL BRIDGE (FIXING THE 0-CHUNK GAP)

The local "Dreaming" motor is idling because the physical memory directories are missing. We need to fuse the external Knowledge Mesh with the local OpenClaw intelligence.

1. **Infrastructure Hardening:** Create the missing memory structure for the entire current squad:
* `mkdir -p /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/memory`
* `mkdir -p ~/.openclaw/agents/{main,artisan,engineer,scientist,guardian}/memory`


2. **The Supabase-to-Local Bridge:** Architect a script (Python or Node.js) to export the 8,236 nodes from Supabase/Chroma and inject them into the local `/memory` directory as Markdown/JSON corpus files.
3. **Bootstrap Indexing:** Force the first `pnpm openclaw memory index` to hydrate the local SQLite/Vector store and confirm `pnpm openclaw memory status` matches the neural density (>8k nodes).

## PHASE 2: AGENT ARMY SCALABILITY (THE PANTHEON ARCHITECTURE)

We are transitioning from a squad of 5 to a **Departmental Army**. Adjust `~/.openclaw/agents/main` and the root config to support this hierarchy:

* **Departmental Heads (L1):** * `Head of Engineering` (Owner of SWEBOK/Infra/Self-healing).
* `Head of Marketing & Growth` (Owner of GCRUX Monetization/Apollo/Affiliates).
* `Head of Research (Scientist)` (Owner of Knowledge Mesh/BOK Scribe).
* `Head of Product (Artisan)` (Owner of UX/Canvas Studio).


* **Sub-Agent Orchestration (L2):** * Implement a **Modular Soul Template** where sub-agents inherit the BoK (Body of Knowledge) of their Department Head but have specific, narrow `Decision Scopes`.
* Automate the `agents add` protocol for new recruits to ensure they immediately point to the canonical `/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra` workspace.



## PHASE 3: AUTONOMIC "NIGHTLY DREAMING" ALIGNMENT

Configure the native OpenClaw Cron to execute the **Conceptual Fine-tuning** every day at 03:00 AM:

* **Task:** The Guardian parses the daily deltas (commits/logs) + new Memory Ingestion.
* **Self-Optimization:** Update `CLAUDE.md` and agent instructions based on the "Nightly Dream" insights.
* **Output:** Morning Briefing on Telegram via `@claw_gcrux`.

---

## IMMEDIATE EXECUTION STEPS:

1. **Audit the `dist` health:** Ensure `pnpm build` is clean to avoid "Module not found" errors.
2. **Register the Guardian:** `pnpm openclaw agents add guardian` to house the Dreaming logs.
3. **Execute the Bridge:** Pave the way for Phase 4.2b (Stripe/Webhooks) by ensuring the agents "know" everything we've built so far.

**"Set-and-forget is the goal. Neural density is the fuel. Scale is the directive."**

> 40	};
> 41	
> 42	// Fetch nodes with null embedding (paginated)
> 43	async function fetchNullEmbeddingNodes(): Promise<Array<{ id: string; name: string }>> {
> 44	  let all: Array<{ id: string; name: string }> = [];
> 45	  let page = 0;
> 46	  const pageSize = 1000;
> 47	  while (true) {
> 48	    const from = page * pageSize;
> 49	    const to = (page + 1) * pageSize - 1;
> 50	    const res = await fetch(
> 51	      `${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?embedding=is.null&select=id,name&offset=${from}&limit=${pageSize}`,
> 52	      { headers: { ...headers, Range: `${from}-${to}`, 'Range-Unit': 'items' } },
> 53	    );
> 54	    if (!res.ok) {
> 55	      console.error('Failed to fetch nodes:', await res.text());
> 56	      process.exit(1);
> 57	    }
> 58	    const data: Array<{ id: string; name: string }> = await res.json();
> 59	    all = all.concat(data);
> 60	    if (data.length < pageSize) break;
> 61	    page++;
> 62	  }
> 63	  return all;
> 64	}
> 65	
> 66	const nodes = await fetchNullEmbeddingNodes();
> 67	console.log(`🔍 Found ${nodes.length} nodes without embeddings.`);
> 68	
> 69	if (nodes.length === 0) {
> 70	  console.log('✅ All nodes already have embeddings.');
> 71	  process.exit(0);
> 72	}
> 73	
> 74	let ok = 0;
> 75	let fail = 0;
> 76	
> 77	for (const node of nodes) {
> 78	  const embedRes = await fetch(`${FUNCTIONS_URL}/embed-mcorch-node`, {
> 79	    method: 'POST',
> 80	    headers,
> 81	    body: JSON.stringify({ node_id: node.id }),
> 82	  });
> 83	
> 84	  if (embedRes.ok) {
> 85	    const result = await embedRes.json();
> 86	    process.stdout.write(`\r  ✅ ${ok + 1}/${nodes.length} embedded — last: ${node.name.slice(0, 30)}`);
> 87	    ok++;
> 88	  } else {
> 89	    const err = await embedRes.text();
> 90	    console.error(`\n  ✗ ${node.name} (${node.id.slice(0, 8)}) — ${err.slice(0, 120)}`);
> 91	    fail++;
> 92	  }
> 93	
> 94	  // Rate-limit: ~2 req/s to avoid OpenRouter quota
> 95	  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));
> 96	}
> 97	
> 98	console.log(`\n\n🏁 Done: ${ok} embedded · ${fail} failed`);
> 99	if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
> 100

> ## Contexto

> Sprint v6.3.0 (Canvas 4.2a) está selada. O motor de "Dreaming" existe mas não tem combustível: a Knowledge Mesh tem 7466+ nós embedados em Supabase mas NÃO há ponte para os SQLite locais que o indexer OpenClaw consome.
> 
> Esta sprint:
> 1. **Constrói a ponte Supabase→disco→indexer OpenClaw** (Two-tier hybrid: rich/AST).
> 2. **Escala o squad para 6 agentes** com Departmental Army (4 Heads L1 + main synthesizer + guardian dreaming-watcher) usando overlay shared + per-agent.
> 3. **Sela o loop Nightly Dreaming completo** (bridge refresh 02:30 → dream nativo 03:00 → morning briefing Telegram 03:30).
> 
> **BoK Gate:** OpenClaw segue com BoK 0/9 (override consciente registrado em v6.2.0, SLA 7d em curso, scientist agent = owner). Esta sprint **expande** o escopo OpenClaw — recomendado disparar `/bok-scribe openclaw` como follow-up imediato após a seal (não bloqueia esta execução, mas zera a janela do SLA).

> ## Phase 1 — Neural Bridge (Two-tier Hybrid)
> 
> ### 1.1 Infraestrutura física
> 
> ```bash
> # Workspace bridge output (master corpus)
> mkdir -p /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/memory/mcorch-export/{tier1,tier2,_shared,_overlays,_meta}
> 
> # Per-agent memory input dirs (consumidos pelo indexer)
> mkdir -p /home/ubuntu/.openclaw/agents/{main,artisan,engineer,scientist,marketing-growth,guardian}/memory
> ```
> 
> Adicionar entrada em `.gitignore` para `memory/mcorch-export/` (corpus regenerável; não versionar 8k+ arquivos).
> 
> ### 1.2 Bridge script — `scripts/bridge-mesh-to-openclaw.ts`
> 
> **Mirror direto do padrão de** `scripts/backfill-embeddings.ts:1-63` (loader `.env` próprio + REST pagination com Range headers + auth via `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`).
> 
> **Schema-source:** `mcorch_nodes` (id, node_type, name, content, metadata jsonb, stability_score, revenue_impact, user_id, project_id, created_at, last_seen_at) + `mcorch_edges` (source_id, target_id, relation_type, weight, metadata).
> 
> **Two-tier classification** (por `node_type`):
> 
> | Tier | node_types | Output | Conteúdo |
> |------|-----------|--------|----------|
> | **Tier 1 (Strategy)** | `decision`, `milestone`, `architecture`, `requirement`, `handoff`, `observation`, `documentation_suite`, `system`, `conversation` | `memory/mcorch-export/tier1/<type>/<id>.md` | YAML frontmatter completo + `content` body integral |
> | **Tier 2 (Symbols)** | `file`, `function`, `arrow_function`, `interface` (AST) | `memory/mcorch-export/tier2/<type>/<id>.md` | YAML frontmatter + apenas `path` + `signature` (de `metadata`) + `summary` (primeiros 200 chars de `content`) |
> 
> **Edges:** `memory/mcorch-export/_meta/edges.jsonl` (uma aresta por linha).
> 
> **Manifest:** `memory/mcorch-export/_meta/manifest.json` com `{ exported_at, tier1_count, tier2_count, edge_count, source_total }` para auditoria.
> 
> **Pagination:** loop com `pageSize=1000`, `Range: ${from}-${to}` + `Range-Unit: items` (idêntico ao backfill).
> 
> **Skip embeddings:** OpenClaw re-embeda localmente; descartar coluna `embedding` do select.
> 
> **Filtros adicionais:** Tier 2 só exporta `metadata->>language IS NOT NULL` (skipa AST mal-formado); ambos pulam `stability_score < 0.05` (FSRS-decayed esquecidos).
> 
> ### 1.3 Overlay distribution — `scripts/distribute-mesh-to-agents.ts`
> 
> Após o bridge produzir o master corpus, popula per-agent input dirs com **shared + agent-specific overlay** (resposta confirmada do Sovereign).
> 
> **Shared base** (symlink → todos os 6 agentes):
> - `tier1/decision/`, `tier1/milestone/`, `tier1/system/`, `tier1/handoff/`
> 
> **Overlays específicos** (symlink → agente apropriado):
> 
> | Agente | Overlay |
> |--------|---------|
> | `engineer` | + `tier1/architecture/` + `tier1/observation/` + **todo `tier2/`** |
> | `scientist` | + `tier1/documentation_suite/` + `tier1/requirement/` + `tier1/conversation/` + `tier1/observation/` |
> | `artisan` | + `tier1/conversation/` + filtro `metadata->tags ?? '' LIKE '%ui%' OR '%ux%'` em `architecture` |
> | `marketing-growth` | + filtro `revenue_impact > 0` em qualquer tier1 + filtro `metadata->tags LIKE '%affiliate%' OR '%apollo%' OR '%gcrux%'` |
> | `main` | + **tudo de tier1** (synthesizer view) |
> | `guardian` | + `tier1/handoff/` + `tier1/milestone/` + `tier1/observation/` (Dreaming logs view) |
> 
> Implementação: symlinks relativos (`ln -sf`) para evitar duplicação física; cada agente vê só o que importa via `~/.openclaw/agents/<id>/memory/<symlink>`.
> 
> ### 1.4 Bootstrap indexing
> 
> ```bash
> # Por agente (loop em script wrapper)
> for agent in main artisan engineer scientist marketing-growth guardian; do
>   pnpm openclaw memory index --agent="$agent"
> done
> 
> # Verificação
> pnpm openclaw memory status  # esperado: chunks > 6000 por agente (varia por overlay)
> ```
> 
> Aceitação: cada SQLite em `~/.openclaw/memory/<agent>.sqlite` reporta `chunks > 1000` (mínimo para shared base + overlay típico). Engineer terá maior densidade (~6k+ com Tier 2 incluído).

> 6. **Manual dream trigger:** `pnpm openclaw memory promote --agent=engineer` (ou equivalente do CLI) — validar `~/.openclaw/agents/engineer/MEMORY.md` ganha entradas novas.
> 7. **Manual morning briefing:** `bash scripts/morning-briefing.sh` — validar mensagem chega em `@claw_gcrux` no Telegram + `/var/log/mcorch-nightly-dream.log` ganha entry de sucesso.
> 8. **Cron sanity:** `crontab -l` mostra as 2 linhas novas. Aguardar 24h para validar execução autônoma do ciclo completo.
> 9. **Failure path:** simular falha no Supabase (env var errada temporariamente) + rodar bridge — validar incident JSON em `/tmp/openclaw-incidents/` + POST tentado ao gateway.
> 10. **TS check:** `npx tsc --noEmit` — zero erros após adicionar os scripts novos.
> 11. **BoK Gate follow-up:** disparar `/bok-scribe openclaw` em sessão dedicada (não bloqueia esta seal, mas zera o SLA 7d do override consciente).
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Sequência de execução proposta
> 
> 1. Phase 1.1 (mkdir) — 1min
> 2. Phase 1.2 (bridge script + dry-run) — ~30min
> 3. Phase 1.3 (distribute) — ~15min
> 4. Phase 1.4 (indexing) — ~10min
> 5. Phase 2.1-2.2 (openclaw.json + 2 SOULs) — ~25min
> 6. Phase 2.3-2.4 (template + CLAUDE.md) — ~20min
> 7. Phase 3.1-3.4 (cron pipeline + scripts) — ~45min
> 8. Verification ponta-a-ponta — ~30min
> 9. `/handoff` com seal v6.3.1 + milestone node + commits granulares — ~15min
> 
> **Total estimado: 3-3.5h de execução focada.**
> 
> ```

function fetchAll<T>(table: string, select: string, extraQuery = ''): Promise<T[]> {\n  const all: T[] = [];\n  let page = 0;\n  const pageSize = 1000;\n  while (true) {\n    const from = page * pageSize;\n    const to = (page + 1) * pageSize - 1;\n    const url = `${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/${table}?select=${select}${extraQuery}&offset=${from}&limit=${pageSize}`;\n    const res = await fetch(url, {\n      headers: { ...headers, Range: `${from}-${to}`, 'Range-Unit': 'items' },\n    });\n    if (!res.ok) {\n      console.error(`Failed to fetch ${table} page ${page}:`, await res.text());\n      process.exit(1);\n    }\n    const data = (await res.json()) as T[];\n    all.push(...data);\n    if (data.length < pageSize) break;\n    page++;\n  }\n  return all;\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction frontmatter(node: Node): string {\n  // Escape values containing newlines/quotes; use JSON.stringify for safety on strings\n  const esc = (v: unknown): string => {\n    if (v === null || v === undefined) return 'null';\n    if (typeof v === 'number' || typeof v === 'boolean') return String(v);\n    if (typeof v === 'string') {\n      // Use JSON-quoted string for safety (valid YAML for strings)\n      return JSON.stringify(v);\n    }\n    return JSON.stringify(v);\n  };\n\n  return `---\nid: ${esc(node.id)}\ntype: ${esc(node.node_type)}\nname: ${esc(node.name)}\nproject_id: ${esc(node.project_id)}\nuser_id: ${esc(node.user_id)}\nstability_score: ${esc(node.stability_score)}\nrevenue_impact: ${esc(node.revenue_impact)}\ncreated_at: ${esc(node.created_at)}\nlast_seen_at: ${esc(node.last_seen_at)}\n---\n\n`;\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Tier 2 summary helper \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction tier2Body(node: Node): string {\n  const meta = node.metadata ?? {};\n  const path = (meta as Record<string, unknown>).path ?? (meta as Record<string, unknown>).file_path ?? '(unknown path)';\n  const signature = (meta as Record<string, unknown>).signature ?? (meta as Record<string, unknown>).type_signature ?? '';\n  const summary = (node.content ?? '').slice(0, 200);\n  return `# ${node.name}\n\n**Path:** \\`${path}\\`\n\n${signature ? `**Signature:** \\`${signature}\\`\\n\\n` : ''}${summary ? `${summary}${(node.content ?? '').length > 200 ? '...' : ''}` : '_(no summary available)_'}\n`;\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Sanitize filename (UUID is already safe; this is defensive) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction safeFilename(id: string): string {\n  return id.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, '_') + '.md';\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Main \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nasync function main() {\n  console.log(`\ud83c\udf09 Bridge mcorch_nodes \u2192 ${EXPORT_ROOT}`);\n  console.log(`   dry-run: ${isDryRun}  tier: ${tierFlag ?? 'all'}  since: ${sinceFlag ?? 'all-time'}`);\n\n  // Build query filters\n  let nodeFilter = '&stability_score=gt.0.05';\n  if (sinceFlag) {\n    // Convert \"24 hours ago\" / \"1 day ago\" to ISO via shell \u2014 simpler: accept ISO directly\n    // For now, support only ISO-style or PostgreSQL \"now() - interval\" via raw clause\n    // Pattern: --since=\"2026-05-15T00:00:00Z\"\n    nodeFilter += `&created_at=gte.${encodeURIComponent(sinceFlag)}`;\n  }\n\n  // Fetch nodes (skip embedding column to reduce payload)\n  const NODE_SELECT = 'id,node_type,name,content,metadata,stability_score,revenue_impact,user_id,project_id,created_at,last_seen_at';\n  console.log('  fetching mcorch_nodes...');\n  const nodes = await fetchAll<Node>('mcorch_nodes', NODE_SELECT, nodeFilter);\n  console.log(`  \u2192 ${nodes.length} nodes fetched`);\n\n  // Classify\n  const buckets: Record<string, Node[]> = { tier1: [], tier2: [], skip: [] };\n  for (const node of nodes) {\n    buckets[classifyTier(node.node_type)].push(node);\n  }\n  console.log(`  \u2192 tier1=${buckets.tier1.length}  tier2=${buckets.tier2.length}  skipped=${buckets.skip.length}`);\n\n  // Per-type counts (helpful for verification)\n  const tier1Counts: Record<string, number> = {};\n  const tier2Counts: Record<string, number> = {};\n  for (const n of buckets.tier1) tier1Counts[n.node_type] = (tier1Counts[n.node_type] ?? 0) + 1;\n  for (const n of buckets.tier2) tier2Counts[n.node_type] = (tier2Counts[n.node_type] ?? 0) + 1;\n  console.log(`  \u2192 tier1 by type:`, tier1Counts);\n  console.log(`  \u2192 tier2 by type:`, tier2Counts);\n\n  // Edges\n  const EDGE_SELECT = 'id,source_id,target_id,relation_type,weight,metadata';\n  console.log('  fetching mcorch_edges...');\n  const edges = await fetchAll<Edge>('mcorch_edges', EDGE_SELECT);\n  console.log(`  \u2192 ${edges.length} edges fetched`);\n\n  if (isDryRun) {\n    console.log('\\n\u2705 Dry-run complete (no files written).');\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Filter by --tier flag\n  const tiersToWrite: Array<'tier1' | 'tier2'> =\n    tierFlag === '1' ? ['tier1'] : tierFlag === '2' ? ['tier2'] : ['tier1', 'tier2'];\n\n  // Idempotency: clean only the tier dirs we're about to write (preserve other tiers if filtered)\n  for (const t of tiersToWrite) {\n    const dir = join(EXPORT_ROOT, t);\n    if (existsSync(dir)) rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });\n    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });\n  }\n\n  // Write Tier 1 files\n  if (tiersToWrite.includes('tier1')) {\n    console.log('  writing tier1...');\n    for (const node of buckets.tier1) {\n      const dir = join(EXPORT_ROOT, 'tier1', node.node_type);\n      mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });\n      const body = `${frontmatter(node)}# ${node.name}\\n\\n${node.content ?? '_(no content)_'}\\n`;\n      writeFileSync(join(dir, safeFilename(node.id)), body);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Write Tier 2 files\n  if (tiersToWrite.includes('tier2')) {\n    console.log('  writing tier2...');\n    for (const node of buckets.tier2) {\n      // Skip Tier 2 nodes with no useful metadata (mal-formed AST entries)\n      const meta = node.metadata ?? {};\n      const language = (meta as Record<string, unknown>).language;\n      if (!language && !(meta as Record<string, unknown>).path && !(meta as Record<string, unknown>).file_path) {\n        continue; // skip mal-formed\n      }\n      const dir = join(EXPORT_ROOT, 'tier2', node.node_type);\n      mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });\n      const body = `${frontmatter(node)}${tier2Body(node)}`;\n      writeFileSync(join(dir, safeFilename(node.id)), body);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Write edges JSONL\n  const metaDir = join(EXPORT_ROOT, '_meta');\n  mkdirSync(metaDir, { recursive: true });\n  const edgeJsonl = edges.map((e) => JSON.stringify(e)).join('\\n') + '\\n';\n  writeFileSync(join(metaDir, 'edges.jsonl'), edgeJsonl);\n\n  // Write manifest\n  const manifest = {\n    exported_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n    source_total: nodes.length,\n    tier1_count: buckets.tier1.length,\n    tier2_count: buckets.tier2.length,\n    skipped: buckets.skip.length,\n    edge_count: edges.length,\n    tier1_by_type: tier1Counts,\n    tier2_by_type: tier2Counts,\n    filters: {\n      since: sinceFlag ?? null,\n      tier: tierFlag ?? null,\n      stability_floor: 0.05,\n    },\n  };\n  writeFileSync(join(metaDir, 'manifest.json'), JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2));\n\n  console.log(`\\n\u2705 Bridge complete. Manifest: ${join(metaDir, 'manifest.json')}`);\n}\n\nawait main();\n"
}

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bun\n/**\n * Distribute the master mesh corpus to per-agent OpenClaw memory dirs via symlinks.\n *\n * Reads from: /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/memory/mcorch-export/\n * Writes to:  /home/ubuntu/.openclaw/agents/<id>/memory/   (one symlink per overlay slice)\n *\n * Distribution model: shared base + agent-specific overlay (Sovereign decision, v6.3.1).\n *\n *   shared base (all 6 agents): tier1/decision \u00b7 tier1/milestone \u00b7 tier1/system \u00b7 tier1/handoff\n *\n *   per-agent overlays:\n *     main             \u2014 full tier1 (synthesizer view)\n *     guardian         \u2014 tier1/handoff + tier1/milestone + tier1/observation (dreaming logs)\n *     engineer         \u2014 tier1/architecture + tier1/observation + ALL tier2\n *     scientist        \u2014 tier1/documentation_suite + tier1/requirement + tier1/conversation + tier1/observation\n *     artisan          \u2014 tier1/conversation + tier1/ui-ux (UX-tagged content)\n *     marketing-growth \u2014 tier1/feature + revenue-flagged Tier 1 (filtered subset, materialized as files)\n *\n * Note: `main` agent's workspace IS the constellation repo, so its indexer reads\n * from `/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/memory/` directly. We still link\n * the export root there explicitly so `main` sees ALL tier1 content.\n *\n * Usage: bun run scripts/distribute-mesh-to-agents.ts\n */\n\nimport { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, symlinkSync, unlinkSync, lstatSync, writeFileSync, rmSync } from 'fs';\nimport { join, dirname, relative } from 'path';\nimport { fileURLToPath } from 'url';\n\nconst __dirname = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst REPO_ROOT = join(__dirname, '..');\nconst EXPORT_ROOT = join(REPO_ROOT, 'memory', 'mcorch-export');\nconst OPENCLAW_AGENTS = '/home/ubuntu/.openclaw/agents';\n\n// Sanity\nif (!existsSync(EXPORT_ROOT)) {\n  console.error(`\u274c Export root missing: ${EXPORT_ROOT}`);\n  console.error('   Run: bun run scripts/bridge-mesh-to-openclaw.ts');\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\n// Read manifest to know what's available\nconst manifestPath = join(EXPORT_ROOT, '_meta', 'manifest.json');\nif (!existsSync(manifestPath)) {\n  console.error(`\u274c Manifest missing: ${manifestPath}`);\n  process.exit(1);\n}\nconst manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(manifestPath, 'utf-8'));\nconsole.log(`\ud83d\udcd6 Manifest: tier1=${manifest.tier1_count}  tier2=${manifest.tier2_count}  exported_at=${manifest.exported_at}`);\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Distribution map \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n// Each agent gets a list of (linkName, sourcePath) pairs.\n// linkName is relative to the agent's memory/ dir.\n// sourcePath is relative to the agent's memory/ dir (for relative symlinks).\n\ninterface AgentDistribution {\n  agent: string;\n  // Symlinks: array of { linkName, target } where target is an absolute path on disk\n  links: Array<{ linkName: string; target: string }>;\n  // Materialized files: array of (filename, contents) for filtered subsets\n  files?: Array<{ filename: string; content: string }>;\n}\n\nconst TIER1 = (subdir: string) => join(EXPORT_ROOT, 'tier1', subdir);\nconst TIER2_ALL = join(EXPORT_ROOT, 'tier2');\n\n// Shared base (4 dirs all agents see)\nconst SHARED_BASE: Array<{ linkName: string; target: string }> = [\n  { linkName: 'shared-decision', target: TIER1('decision') },\n  { linkName: 'shared-milestone', target: TIER1('milestone') },\n  { linkName: 'shared-system', target: TIER1('system') },\n  { linkName: 'shared-handoff', target: TIER1('handoff') },\n];\n\nconst distributions: AgentDistribution[] = [\n  {\n    agent: 'main',\n    links: [\n      ...SHARED_BASE,\n      // main = synthesizer: sees full tier1\n      { linkName: 'overlay-tier1-full', target: join(EXPORT_ROOT, 'tier1') },\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    agent: 'guardian',\n    links: [\n      ...SHARED_BASE,\n      { linkName: 'overlay-observation', target: TIER1('observation') },\n      // guardian also sees the export manifest itself\n      { linkName: 'overlay-meta', target: join(EXPORT_ROOT, '_meta') },\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    agent: 'engineer',\n    links: [\n      ...SHARED_BASE,\n      { linkName: 'overlay-architecture', target: TIER1('architecture') },\n      { linkName: 'overlay-observation', target: TIER1('observation') },\n      // engineer gets the FULL Tier 2 symbol map\n      { linkName: 'overlay-tier2-symbols', target: TIER2_ALL },\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    agent: 'scientist',\n    links: [\n      ...SHARED_BASE,\n      { linkName: 'overlay-documentation-suite', target: TIER1('documentation_suite') },\n      { linkName: 'overlay-requirement', target: TIER1('requirement') },\n      { linkName: 'overlay-conversation', target: TIER1('conversation') },\n      { linkName: 'overlay-observation', target: TIER1('observation') },\n      { linkName: 'overlay-vault-note', target: TIER1('vault_note') },\n      { linkName: 'overlay-markdown-file', target: TIER1('markdown_file') },\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    agent: 'artisan',\n    links: [\n      ...SHARED_BASE,\n      { linkName: 'overlay-conversation', target: TIER1('conversation') },\n      { linkName: 'overlay-ui-ux', target: TIER1('ui-ux') },\n      { linkName: 'overlay-feature', target: TIER1('feature') },\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    agent: 'marketing-growth',\n    links: [\n      ...SHARED_BASE,\n      { linkName: 'overlay-feature', target: TIER1('feature') },\n      // revenue-flagged subset is materialized below as filtered files\n    ],\n    // Materialize a \"revenue-flagged\" overlay by scanning Tier 1 markdown files\n    // and writing only those with `revenue_impact: <not null and > 0>` into a dedicated dir.\n    files: collectRevenueFlagged(),\n  },\n];\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Helpers \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\nfunction collectRevenueFlagged(): Array<{ filename: string; content: string }> {\n  const collected: Array<{ filename: string; content: string }> = [];\n  const tier1Root = join(EXPORT_ROOT, 'tier1');\n  if (!existsSync(tier1Root)) return collected;\n  for (const typeDir of readdirSync(tier1Root)) {\n    const typePath = join(tier1Root, typeDir);\n    if (!lstatSync(typePath).isDirectory()) continue;\n    for (const file of readdirSync(typePath)) {\n      const fpath = join(typePath, file);\n      const content = readFileSync(fpath, 'utf-8');\n      // Frontmatter parse: look for `revenue_impact: <number>` where number > 0\n      const m = content.match(/^revenue_impact:\\s*([0-9.]+)/m);\n      if (m && parseFloat(m[1]) > 0) {\n        collected.push({ filename: `revenue-${typeDir}-${file}`, content });\n      }\n      // Tag-based filter: scan for affiliate/apollo/gcrux mentions in content body\n      const lower = content.toLowerCase();\n      if (\n        !m && // don't double-count\n        (lower.includes('affiliate') ||\n          lower.includes('apollo') ||\n          lower.includes('gcrux') ||\n          lower.includes('mercado livre') ||\n          lower.includes('mercadolivre'))\n      ) {\n        collected.push({ filename: `tagged-${typeDir}-${file}`, content });\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return collected;\n}\n\nfunction clearOverlayLinks(memoryDir: string): void {\n  if (!existsSync(memoryDir)) return;\n  for (const entry of readdirSync(memoryDir)) {\n    if (!entry.startsWith('shared-') && !entry.startsWith('overlay-') && !entry.startsWith('revenue-flagged')) {\n      continue; // never touch user-created files\n    }\n    const p = join(memoryDir, entry);\n    const st = lstatSync(p);\n    if (st.isSymbolicLink() || st.isFile()) {\n      unlinkSync(p);\n    } else if (st.isDirectory()) {\n      rmSync(p, { recursive: true, force: true });\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction ensureDir(p: string): void {\n  mkdirSync(p, { recursive: true });\n}\n\nfunction makeRelativeSymlink(linkPath: string, target: string): void {\n  // Compute relative path from linkPath's parent dir to target\n  const rel = relative(dirname(linkPath), target);\n  symlinkSync(rel, linkPath);\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Apply distribution \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfor (const dist of distributions) {\n  const memDir = join(OPENCLAW_AGENTS, dist.agent, 'memory');\n  console.log(`\\n\ud83c\udfaf ${dist.agent} \u2192 ${memDir}`);\n  ensureDir(memDir);\n  clearOverlayLinks(memDir);\n\n  // Symlinks\n  for (const { linkName, target } of dist.links) {\n    if (!existsSync(target)) {\n      console.log(`   \u26a0\ufe0f  skip ${linkName} (target missing: ${target})`);\n      continue;\n    }\n    const linkPath = join(memDir, linkName);\n    makeRelativeSymlink(linkPath, target);\n    console.log(`   \u2713 ${linkName} \u2192 ${relative(memDir, target)}`);\n  }\n\n  // Materialized files (e.g. revenue-flagged for marketing-growth)\n  if (dist.files && dist.files.length > 0) {\n    const filesDir = join(memDir, 'revenue-flagged');\n    ensureDir(filesDir);\n    for (const { filename, content } of dist.files) {\n      writeFileSync(join(filesDir, filename), content);\n    }\n    console.log(`   \u2713 revenue-flagged/ \u2192 ${dist.files.length} materialized files`);\n  }\n}\n\nconsole.log('\\n\u2705 Distribution complete.');\n"
}

> 1	# AGENTS.md - Your Workspace
> 2	
> 3	This folder is home. Treat it that way.

> 5	## First Run
> 6	
> 7	If `BOOTSTRAP.md` exists, that's your birth certificate. Follow it, figure out who you are, then delete it. You won't need it again.
> 8	
> 9	## Session Startup
> 10	
> 11	Use runtime-provided startup context first.
> 12	
> 13	That context may already include:
> 14	
> 15	- `AGENTS.md`, `SOUL.md`, and `USER.md`
> 16	- recent daily memory such as `memory/YYYY-MM-DD.md`
> 17	- `MEMORY.md` when this is the main session
> 18	
> 19	Do not manually reread startup files unless:
> 20	
> 21	1. The user explicitly asks
> 22	2. The provided context is missing something you need
> 23	3. You need a deeper follow-up read beyond the provided startup context
> 24	
> 25	## Memory
> 26	
> 27	You wake up fresh each session. These files are your continuity:
> 28	
> 29	- **Daily notes:** `memory/YYYY-MM-DD.md` (create `memory/` if needed) — raw logs of what happened
> 30	- **Long-term:** `MEMORY.md` — your curated memories, like a human's long-term memory
> 31	
> 32	Capture what matters. Decisions, context, things to remember. Skip the secrets unless asked to keep them.
> 33	
> 34	### 🧠 MEMORY.md - Your Long-Term Memory
> 35	
> 36	- **ONLY load in main session** (direct chats with your human)
> 37	- **DO NOT load in shared contexts** (Discord, group chats, sessions with other people)
> 38	- This is for **security** — contains personal context that shouldn't leak to strangers
> 39	- You can **read, edit, and update** MEMORY.md freely in main sessions
> 40	- Write significant events, thoughts, decisions, opinions, lessons learned
> 41	- This is your curated memory — the distilled essence, not raw logs
> 42	- Over time, review your daily files and update MEMORY.md with what's worth keeping
> 43	
> 44	### 📝 Write It Down - No "Mental Notes"!
> 45	
> 46	- **Memory is limited** — if you want to remember something, WRITE IT TO A FILE
> 47	- "Mental notes" don't survive session restarts. Files do.
> 48	- When someone says "remember this" → update `memory/YYYY-MM-DD.md` or relevant file
> 49	- When you learn a lesson → update AGENTS.md, TOOLS.md, or the relevant skill
> 50	- When you make a mistake → document it so future-you doesn't repeat it
> 51	- **Text > Brain** 📝
> 52	
> 53	## Red Lines
> 54	
> 55	- Don't exfiltrate private data. Ever.
> 56	- Don't run destructive commands without asking.
> 57	- `trash` > `rm` (recoverable beats gone forever)
> 58	- When in doubt, ask.
> 59	
> 60	## External vs Internal

SWEBOK \u00b7 CMQ/OE lens \u00b7 Nightly Dreaming orchestrator \u00b7 alignment auditor.\n> Workspace: `/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra`\n\n## Purpose\n\nFechar a malha auton\u00f4mica via observa\u00e7\u00e3o meta: orquestra o ciclo Nightly Dreaming (03:00 BRT), sintetiza deltas das \u00faltimas 24h (commits + logs + n\u00f3s novos do mesh + incidents) em um morning briefing entregue via Telegram `@claw_gcrux`, e detecta padr\u00f5es emergentes que justificam atualiza\u00e7\u00e3o de `CLAUDE.md` \u2014 sempre via proposta PR-style, nunca commit direto. \u00c9 a \u00fanica entidade do squad cuja fun\u00e7\u00e3o prim\u00e1ria \u00e9 \"observar o sistema observar a si mesmo\".\n\n## Core Capabilities\n\n1. **Daily Git Delta Parsing** \u2014 Roda `git log --since=\"24 hours ago\"` no workspace can\u00f4nico, classifica commits (feat/fix/refactor/docs/chore) e extrai m\u00f3dulos tocados.\n2. **MEMORY.md Aggregation** \u2014 L\u00ea `~/.openclaw/agents/*/MEMORY.md` (todos os 6 agentes), identifica entradas novas das \u00faltimas 24h, calcula entropy (quantos agentes promoveram entradas \u2192 sinal de alta agita\u00e7\u00e3o no sistema).\n3. **Morning Briefing Composition** \u2014 Combina (a) git delta \u00b7 (b) memory deltas \u00b7 (c) BoK debt status \u00b7 (d) revenue snippet do marketing-growth \u00b7 (e) infra incidents (`/tmp/openclaw-incidents/` \u00faltimos 24h \u00b7 `infra_health_logs` rows recentes) em mensagem markdown \u2264 4096 chars (limite Telegram).\n4. **Self-Optimization Detection** \u2014 Quando padr\u00e3o emerge (3+ ocorr\u00eancias de mesmo tipo de fix \u00b7 novo m\u00f3dulo entrando no roadmap \u00b7 agente que repete erro), prop\u00f5e edit a `CLAUDE.md` em arquivo `.claude/context/proposed-claude-md-<YYYY-MM-DD>.md` formato PR diff. NUNCA edita CLAUDE.md direto \u2014 Sovereign aprova explicitamente.\n5. **Dream Cycle Watchdog** \u2014 Verifica que o OpenClaw `Memory Dreaming Promotion` cron (03:00) executou sem erros. Em failure, escala via incident JSON em `/tmp/openclaw-incidents/` + POST best-effort ao gateway (mirror do padr\u00e3o `scripts/watchdog-mcorch.sh:69-131`).\n\n## Skills (bindings)\n\n- `git` \u2014 log/diff/branch parsing\n- `openclaw message send` \u2014 Telegram delivery via OpenClaw bridge\n- `supabase-mcp` \u2014 leitura de `infra_health_logs` \u00b7 contagem de n\u00f3s novos no mesh (`created_at > now() - interval '24h'`)\n- `bash` \u2014 runbook execution\n- Comando interno: aciona `scripts/compose-morning-briefing.ts` (TS script)\n\n## Inputs\n\n- `~/.openclaw/agents/*/MEMORY.md` (deltas \u2014 todos os agentes)\n- `git log --since=\"24 hours ago\" --pretty=format:'%h %s'` (workspace can\u00f4nico)\n- `mcorch_nodes.created_at` recente (Supabase quando self-optimization \u00e9 proposta\n- Knowledge Mesh: n\u00f3 `observation` por morning briefing executado (auditoria)\n\n## Decision Scope\n\n- \u2705 Decide o que entra no morning briefing (prioriza\u00e7\u00e3o + concis\u00e3o)\n- \u2705 Decide quando propor edit a CLAUDE.md (com justificativa em texto)\n- \u2705 Decide quando escalar dream cycle failure como incident\n- \u274c N\u00c3O commita edits a CLAUDE.md sem aprova\u00e7\u00e3o expl\u00edcita do Sovereign\n- \u274c N\u00c3O escala incidents de infra (esse caminho \u00e9 do Engineer via watchdog)\n- \u274c N\u00c3O   "new_string": "function clearOverlayLinks(memoryDir: string): void {\n  // Idempotency: remove previous shared-*, overlay-*, revenue-flagged dirs (we own these prefixes).\n  if (!existsSync(memoryDir)) return;\n  for (const entry of readdirSync(memoryDir)) {\n    if (!entry.startsWith('shared-') && !entry.startsWith('overlay-') && !entry.startsWith('revenue-flagged')) {\n      continue; // never touch user-created files\n    }\n    rmSync(join(memoryDir, entry), { recursive: true, force: true });\n  }\n}\n\nfunction ensureDir(p: string): void {\n  mkdirSync(p, { recursive: true });\n}\n\n/**\n * Copy all .md files from `sourceDir` into a subdirectory `destName` under `parentDir`.\n * The indexer scans recursively but does NOT follow symlinks, so we materialize copies.\n * Files are flat (no nested dirs) \u2014 sourceDir is expected to contain only .md files.\n */\nfunction copyTreeFlat(sourceDir: string, parentDir: string, destName: string): number {\n  if (!existsSync(sourceDir) || !lstatSync(sourceDir).isDirectory()) return 0;\n  const dest = join(parentDir, destName);\n  ensureDir(dest);\n  let count = 0;\n  for (const entry of readdirSync(sourceDir)) {\n    const src = join(sourceDir, entry);\n    if (!lstatSync(src).isFile() || !entry.endsWith('.md') && !entry.endsWith('.json') && !entry.endsWith('.jsonl')) continue;\n    copyFileSync(src, join(dest, entry));\n    count++;\n  }\n  return count;\n}\n\n/**\n * Recursive variant \u2014 for overlays that point at a directory containing subdirectories\n * (e.g. main's overlay-tier1-full points at tier1/ which contains tier1/decision/, tier1/milestone/, ...).\n * Files are flattened: tier1/decision/abc.md \u2192 dest/decision__abc.md (prefix preserves origin).\n */\nfunction copyTreeRecursive(sourceDir: string, parentDir: string, destName: string): number {\n  if (!existsSync(sourceDir) || !lstatSync(sourceDir).isDirectory()) return 0;\n  const dest = join(parentDir, destName);\n  ensureDir(dest);\n  let count = 0;\n  for (const subEntry of readdirSync(sourceDir)) {\n    const subPath = join(sourceDir, subEntry);\n    const stat = lstatSync(subPath);\n    if (stat.isFile() && (subEntry.endsWith('.md') || subEntry.endsWith('.json') || subEntry.endsWith('.jsonl'))) {\n      copyFileSync(subPath, join(dest, subEntry));\n      count++;\n    } else if (stat.isDirectory()) {\n      for (const innerEntry of readdirSync(subPath)) {\n        const innerPath = join(subPath, innerEntry);\n        if (!lstatSync(innerPath).isFile() || !(innerEntry.endsWith('.md') || innerEntry.endsWith('.json') || innerEntry.endsWith('.jsonl'))) continue;\n        copyFileSync(innerPath, join(dest, `${subEntry}__${innerEntry}`));\n        count++;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return count;\n}"
}

  "new_string": "// \u2500\u2500 Apply distribution \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n// Note: main's workspace IS the constellation repo, so main reads memory/mcorch-export/ directly\n// via the indexer's recursive scan \u2014 we skip the copy dance for main.\nconst SKIP_COPY_FOR_AGENTS = new Set(['main']);\n\nfor (const dist of distributions) {\n  const memDir = join(OPENCLAW_AGENTS, dist.agent, 'memory');\n  console.log(`\\n\ud83c\udfaf ${dist.agent} \u2192 ${memDir}`);\n  ensureDir(memDir);\n  clearOverlayLinks(memDir);\n\n  if (SKIP_COPY_FOR_AGENTS.has(dist.agent)) {\n    console.log(`   \u23e9 skipping (workspace IS the repo; indexer reads mcorch-export/ directly)`);\n    continue;\n  }\n\n  // Materialize file copies (indexer doesn't follow symlinks)\n  for (const { linkName, target } of dist.links) {\n    if (!existsSync(target)) {\n      console.log(`   \u26a0\ufe0f  skip ${linkName} (target missing: ${target})`);\n      continue;\n    }\n    // Decide whether to use flat or recursive copy based on directory shape\n    const isRecursive = linkName === 'overlay-tier1-full' || linkName === 'overlay-tier2-symbols' || linkName === 'overlay-meta';\n    const count = isRecursive\n      ? copyTreeRecursive(target, memDir, linkName)\n      : copyTreeFlat(target, memDir, linkName);\n    console.log(`   \u2713 ${linkName}/ \u2192 ${count} files copied`);\n  }\n\n  // Materialized files (e.g. revenue-flagged for marketing-growth)\n  if (dist.files && dist.files.length > 0) {\n    const filesDir = join(memDir, 'revenue-flagged');\n    ensureDir(filesDir);\n    for (const { filename, content } of dist.files) {\n      writeFileSync(join(filesDir, filename), content);\n    }\n    console.log(`   \u2713 revenue-flagged/ \u2192 ${dist.files.length} materialized files`);\n  }\n}\n\nconsole.log('\\n\u2705 Distribution complete.');"
}

Mirror the Pantheon hierarchy.)\n\n## Operational Constraints\n- <hard constraint 1 \u2014 usually about secrets, rate limits, or financial actions>\n- <hard constraint 2>\n- ...\n\n## Active Debt (<YYYY-MM-DD>)\n1. <Open item> \u2014 owner: <agent> \u00b7 SLA: <days>\n2. ...\n(Updated quarterly or on major sprint. It inherits:\n\n- The parent Head's `Skills (bindings)` (additive \u2014 sub-agent can ADD specific tools)\n- The parent Head's `Decision Scope` \u274c constraints (cannot remove)\n- The parent Head's mesh overlay (sub-agent may filter further but never broaden)\n\nSub-agent must NARROW the Decision Scope \u2705 list to its specialty (e.g. `stripe-migrator` sub-agent under `engineer` only decides Stripe-related actions; cannot touch general infra without `engineer` approval).\n\n**Scaffolding a sub-agent:**\n\n```bash\n# 1. Create the directory\nmkdir -p /home/ubuntu/.openclaw/agents/<sub-agent-id>/{memory,sessions}\n\n# 2. Copy boilerplate from parent Head\nfor f in AGENTS.md HEARTBEAT.md IDENTITY.md TOOLS.md USER.md; do\n  cp /home/ubuntu/.openclaw/agents/<parent-head>/$f \\\n     /home/ubuntu/.openclaw/agents/<sub-agent-id>/$f\ndone\n\n# 3. Write SOUL.md using this template + add `> Parent: <head-id>` preamble line\n\n# 4. Register in /home/ubuntu/.openclaw/openclaw.json under agents.list:\n#    { \"id\": \"<sub-agent-id>\", \"name\": \"<sub-agent-id>\",\n#      \"workspace\": \"/home/ubuntu/.openclaw/agents/<sub-agent-id>\",\n#      \"agentDir\": \"/home/ubuntu/.openclaw/agents/<sub-agent-id>/agent\" }\n\n# 5. Add memory overlay distribution rule to scripts/distribute-mesh-to-agents.ts\n#    (inherit parent's overlay + add narrower filter)\n\n# 6. Run distribute + index\nbun run scripts/distribute-mesh-to-agents.ts\nnode /home/ubuntu/openclaw/openclaw.mjs memory index --agent=\"<sub-agent-id>\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Memory overlay \u2192 SOUL alignment\n\nEach role's overlay (defined in `scripts/distribute-mesh-to-agents.ts`) MUST match the `Inputs` section of the SOUL. If you change one, change the other in the same commit.\n\nSource of truth for the overlay mapping:\n\n- `scripts/distribute-mesh-to-agents.ts` (the script)\n- `CLAUDE.md` \u00a7 \"Departmental Army (Pantheon Architecture)\" (the human-readable contract)\n\n---\n\n## When to update SOULs\n\n- **Add a Core Capability** when the agent ships a new operational pattern that ran successfully 3+ times\n- **Add to Active Debt** when a known limitation is discovered (do NOT silently work around it)\n- **Update Collaboration Map** when a new edge to another agent emerges from real workflow\n- **Bump KPIs** quarterly with measured baselines\n\nSovereign approves all SOUL changes for L1 Heads. L2 sub-agents can self-edit Active Debt and KPIs; structural changes (Purpose, Decision Scope) require Sovereign review.\n\n---\n\n## Template-version compatibility\n\nThis template is anchored to OpenClaw `2026.5.14` and MCORCH Pantheon v6.3.1.\nWhen OpenClaw plugin schema changes (new agent fields, deprecated capabilities), update this file FIRST, then propagate to all SOULs.\n\nLast updated: 2026-05-16 (v6.3.1 \u2014 Departmental Army bootstrap).\n"

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# nightly-bridge-refresh.sh \u2014 Refresh OpenClaw memory from latest Knowledge Mesh state.\n#\n# Pipeline (runs at 02:30 BRT via cron, ahead of the OpenClaw Memory Dreaming Promotion at 03:00):\n#   1. Re-export mcorch_nodes / mcorch_edges \u2192 memory/mcorch-export/\n#   2. Re-distribute corpus to per-agent memory dirs\n#   3. Re-index each agent's memory (incremental)\n#\n# Failure path: write incident JSON + best-effort POST to OpenClaw gateway (mirror of\n# scripts/watchdog-mcorch.sh:69-131). Engineer agent picks up via /tmp/openclaw-incidents/ inbox.\n#\n# Log: /var/log/mcorch-nightly-bridge.log (append-only)\n# Owner: guardian agent (consumes); engineer agent (maintains)\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nREPO=/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra\nLOG=/var/log/mcorch-nightly-bridge.log\nINCIDENT_DIR=/tmp/openclaw-incidents\nGATEWAY_URL=\"http://127.0.0.1:18789/agents/engineer/incidents\"\nOPENCLAW_CLI=/home/ubuntu/openclaw/openclaw.mjs\n\nmkdir -p \"$INCIDENT_DIR\"\n\n# Helper: write incident JSON and POST best-effort to the engineer agent.\nescalate_failure() {\n  local step=\"$1\"\n  local exit_code=\"$2\"\n  local incident_id=\"nightly-bridge-$(date +%s)\"\n  local incident_file=\"$INCIDENT_DIR/${incident_id}.json\"\n  cat > \"$incident_file\" <<JSON\n{\n  \"incident_id\": \"$incident_id\",\n  \"service\": \"nightly-bridge-refresh\",\n  \"step\": \"$step\",\n  \"exit_code\": $exit_code,\n  \"timestamp_iso\": \"$(date -Is)\",\n  \"log_tail\": $(tail -50 \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null | jq -Rs .),\n  \"severity\": \"P1\",\n  \"playbook_hint\": \"see .claude/context/openclaw-self-heal-runbook.md (Layer 2 escalation)\"\n}\nJSON\n  curl -sS -m 5 -X POST \"$GATEWAY_URL\" \\\n    -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n    -d @\"$incident_file\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || \\\n    echo \"[$(date -Is)] gateway POST failed; incident file: $incident_file\" >> \"$LOG\"\n}\n\ntrap 'EC=$?; if [ \"$EC\" -ne 0 ]; then escalate_failure \"trap\" \"$EC\"; fi' EXIT\n\n{\n  echo \"================================================================\"\n  echo \"[$(date -Is)] nightly-bridge-refresh START\"\n  echo \"================================================================\"\n\n  cd \"$REPO\"\n\n  echo \"\u2192 Step 1/3: bridge-mesh-to-openclaw.ts (full export)\"\n  bun run scripts/bridge-mesh-to-openclaw.ts\n\n  echo \"\u2192 Step 2/3: distribute-mesh-to-agents.ts\"\n  bun run scripts/distribute-mesh-to-agents.ts\n\n  echo \"\u2192 Step 3/3: memory index (per agent)\"\n  FAIL_COUNT=0\n  for agent in main artisan engineer scientist marketing-growth guardian; do\n    if ! node \"$OPENCLAW_CLI\" memory index --agent=\"$agent\" 2>&1; then\n      echo \"FAIL indexing $agent\"\n      FAIL_COUNT=$((FAIL_COUNT + 1))\n    fi\n  done\n\n  if [ \"$FAIL_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    echo \"FAIL: $FAIL_COUNT agents failed to index\"\n    escalate_failure \"memory-index\" \"$FAIL_COUNT\"\n    exit 1\n  fi\n\n  echo \"[$(date -Is)] nightly-bridge-refresh DONE (zero failures)\"\n} >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1\n"
}

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# morning-briefing.sh \u2014 Guardian's daily Telegram delivery to @claw_gcrux.\n#\n# Runs at 03:30 BRT (after Memory Dreaming Promotion at 03:00 and nightly-bridge at 02:30).\n# Composes a markdown briefing via scripts/compose-morning-briefing.ts and POSTs to Telegram\n# directly via the Bot API (single source of truth: bot token from ~/.openclaw/openclaw.json).\n#\n# Log: /var/log/mcorch-nightly-dream.log (append-only)\n# Owner: guardian agent\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nREPO=/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra\nLOG=/var/log/mcorch-nightly-dream.log\nOPENCLAW_CONFIG=/home/ubuntu/.openclaw/openclaw.json\nTG_CHAT=\"@claw_gcrux\"\nINCIDENT_DIR=/tmp/openclaw-incidents\nGATEWAY_URL=\"http://127.0.0.1:18789/agents/engineer/incidents\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$INCIDENT_DIR\"\n\nescalate_failure() {\n  local step=\"$1\"\n  local exit_code=\"$2\"\n  local incident_id=\"morning-briefing-$(date +%s)\"\n  cat > \"$INCIDENT_DIR/${incident_id}.json\" <<JSON\n{\n  \"incident_id\": \"$incident_id\",\n  \"service\": \"morning-briefing\",\n  \"step\": \"$step\",\n  \"exit_code\": $exit_code,\n  \"timestamp_iso\": \"$(date -Is)\",\n  \"severity\": \"P1\"\n}\nJSON\n  curl -sS -m 5 -X POST \"$GATEWAY_URL\" \\\n    -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n    -d @\"$INCIDENT_DIR/${incident_id}.json\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || true\n}\n\ntrap 'EC=$?; if [ \"$EC\" -ne 0 ]; then escalate_failure \"trap\" \"$EC\"; fi' EXIT\n\n{\n  echo \"================================================================\"\n  echo \"[$(date -Is)] morning-briefing START\"\n  echo \"================================================================\"\n\n  cd \"$REPO\"\n\n  # Compose markdown briefing (stdout)\n  BRIEF=$(bun run scripts/compose-morning-briefing.ts 2>>\"$LOG\")\n  if [ -z \"$BRIEF\" ]; then\n    echo \"FAIL: compose-morning-briefing produced empty output\"\n    escalate_failure \"compose-empty\" \"1\"\n    exit 1\n  fi\n\n  # Truncate to 4000 chars to leave room for Telegram overhead (limit 4096)\n  if [ \"${#BRIEF}\" -gt 4000 ]; then\n    BRIEF=\"${BRIEF:0:3950}...\\n_[truncated \u2014 full log: $LOG]_\"\n  fi\n\n  TOKEN=$(jq -r '.channels.telegram.botToken' \"$OPENCLAW_CONFIG\")\n  if [ -z \"$TOKEN\" ] || [ \"$TOKEN\" = \"null\" ]; then\n    echo \"FAIL: no Telegram token in $OPENCLAW_CONFIG\"\n    escalate_failure \"no-token\" \"2\"\n    exit 2\n  fi\n\n  # POST to Telegram (Markdown parse_mode)\n  HTTP_CODE=$(curl -sS -o /tmp/morning-briefing-tg-response.json -w \"%{http_code}\" -X POST \\\n    \"https://api.telegram.org/bot${TOKEN}/sendMessage\" \\\n    -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n    -d \"$(jq -n --arg c \"$TG_CHAT\" --arg t \"$BRIEF\" \\\n      '{chat_id:$c, text:$t, parse_mode:\"Markdown\", disable_web_page_preview:true}')\")\n\n  if [ \"$HTTP_CODE\" != \"200\" ]; then\n    echo \"FAIL: Telegram API returned HTTP $HTTP_CODE\"\n    cat /tmp/morning-briefing-tg-response.json\n    escalate_failure \"telegram-api\" \"$HTTP_CODE\"\n    exit 3\n  fi\n\n  echo \"[$(date -Is)] morning-briefing DELIVERED to $TG_CHAT (chars=${#BRIEF})\"\n} >> \"$LOG\" 2>&1\n"
}

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bun\n/**\n * compose-morning-briefing.ts \u2014 Build the daily Guardian briefing as Markdown.\n *\n * Reads:\n *   \u2022 git log --since=\"24 hours ago\"  (commits delta)\n *   \u2022 Supabase: mcorch_nodes created in last 24h  (mesh growth)\n *   \u2022 ~/.openclaw/agents/*/MEMORY.md  (per-agent dream promotions)\n *   \u2022 /tmp/openclaw-incidents/*.json  (last 24h incidents)\n *   \u2022 docs/bok/* completeness (BoK debts with SLA timer)\n *   \u2022 Supabase: pipeline_runs revenue last 24h  (marketing-growth snippet)\n *\n * Output: stdout (markdown, \u2264 4000 chars for Telegram safety).\n * Consumed by: scripts/morning-briefing.sh\n */\n\nimport { existsSync, readFileSync, statSync, readdirSync } from 'fs';\nimport { join, dirname } from 'path';\nimport { fileURLToPath } from 'url';\nimport { execSync } from 'child_process';\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Load .env \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nconst __dirname = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst envPath = join(__dirname, '../.env');\nif (existsSync(envPath)) {\n  for (const line of readFileSync(envPath, 'utf-8').split('\\n')) {\n    const m = line.match(/^([^#=\\s][^=]*)=(.*)$/);\n    if (m) process.env[m[1].trim()] = m[2].trim().replace(/^[\"']|[\"']$/g, '');\n  }\n}\n\nconst SUPABASE_URL = process.env.SUPABASE_URL;\nconst SERVICE_KEY = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;\nconst REPO_ROOT = join(__dirname, '..');\nconst OPENCLAW_AGENTS = '/home/ubuntu/.openclaw/agents';\nconst INCIDENT_DIR = '/tmp/openclaw-incidents';\n\nconst sbHeaders = SUPABASE_URL && SERVICE_KEY\n  ? { apikey: SERVICE_KEY, Authorization: `Bearer ${SERVICE_KEY}` }\n  : null;\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Section helpers \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\nfunction safeExec(cmd: string): string {\n  try {\n    return execSync(cmd, { cwd: REPO_ROOT, encoding: 'utf-8' }).trim();\n  } catch {\n    return '';\n  }\n}\n\nasync function gitDelta(): Promise<string> {\n  const log = safeExec('git log --since=\"24 hours ago\" --pretty=format:\"%h %s\" --no-merges');\n  if (!log) return '_no commits in last 24h_';\n  const lines = log.split('\\n');\n  return lines.slice(0, 10).map((l) => `\u2022 \\`${l}\\``).join('\\n') + (lines.length > 10 ? `\\n\u2022 _+${lines.length - 10} more_` : '');\n}\n\nasync function meshGrowth(): Promise<string> {\n  if (!sbHeaders) return '_(mesh query unavailable \u2014 env missing)_';\n  const since = new Date(Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();\n  try {\n    const url = `${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?select=node_type&created_at=gte.${encodeURIComponent(since)}`;\n    const res = await fetch(url, { headers: sbHeaders });\n    if (!res.ok) return '_(mesh fetch failed)_';\n    const rows = (await res.json()) as Array<{ node_type: string }>;\n    if (rows.length === 0) return '_no new nodes_';\n    const counts: Record<string, number> = {};\n    for (const r of rows) counts[r.node_type] = (counts[r.node_type] ?? 0) + 1;\n    const top = Object.entries(counts).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 5);\n    return `${rows.length} new nodes (${top.map(([t, c]) => `${t}:${c}`).join(', ')})`;\n  } catch {\n    return '_(mesh query error)_';\n  }\n}\n\nfunction memoryPromotions(): string {\n  const agents = ['main', 'artisan', 'engineer', 'scientist', 'marketing-growth', 'guardian'];\n  const lines: string[] = [];\n  for (const agent of agents) {\n    const mem = join(OPENCLAW_AGENTS, agent, 'MEMORY.md');\n    if (!existsSync(mem)) {\n      lines.push(`\u2022 ${agent}: _(no MEMORY.md yet)_`);\n      continue;\n    }\n    const stat = statSync(mem);\n    const ageMs = Date.now() - stat.mtimeMs;\n    const ageH = Math.round(ageMs / (60 * 60 * 1000));\n    if (ageH > 36) {\n      lines.push(`\u2022 ${agent}: _stale (${ageH}h)_`);\n      continue;\n    }\n    // Count recent entries by looking for # / ## headings in last 100 lines\n    const content = readFileSync(mem, 'utf-8');\n    const headings = (content.match(/^#{1,3} /gm) ?? []).length;\n    lines.push(`\u2022 ${agent}: ${headings} entries (updated ${ageH}h ago)`);\n  }\n  return lines.join('\\n');\n}\n\nfunction bokDebts(): string {\n  const bokRoot = join(REPO_ROOT, 'docs', 'bok');\n  if (!existsSync(bokRoot)) return '_no BoK suites_';\n  const required = ['00-index', '01-mrd', '02-brd', '03-prd', '04-frd', '05-sdd', '06-data-model', '07-process-flow', '08-quality-metrics'];\n  const debts: string[] = [];\n  for (const slug of readdirSync(bokRoot)) {\n    const slugDir = join(bokRoot, slug);\n    if (!statSync(slugDir).isDirectory()) continue;\n    const missing = required.filter((d) => !existsSync(join(slugDir, `${d}.md`)));\n    if (missing.length > 0) {\n      debts.push(`\u2022 \\`${slug}\\` (${9 - missing.length}/9) \u2014 missing: ${missing.join(', ')}`);\n    }\n  }\n  // Also check OpenClaw BoK (not in docs/bok by default \u2014 separate slug)\n  const openclawBok = join(bokRoot, 'openclaw');\n  if (!existsSync(openclawBok)) {\n    debts.push('\u2022 `openclaw` (0/9) \u2014 owner: scientist \u00b7 SLA 7d (override v6.2.0)');\n  }\n  return debts.length > 0 ? debts.join('\\n') : '_all BoK suites sealed_';\n}\n\nfunction recentIncidents(): string {\n  if (!existsSync(INCIDENT_DIR)) return '_none_';\n  const cutoff = Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n  const files = readdirSync(INCIDENT_DIR)\n    .filter((f) => f.endsWith('.json'))\n    .map((f) => ({ f, path: join(INCIDENT_DIR, f), mtime: statSync(join(INCIDENT_DIR, f)).mtimeMs }))\n    .filter((x) => x.mtime > cutoff)\n    .sort((a, b) => b.mtime - a.mtime);\n  if (files.length === 0) return '_none in last 24h_';\n  return files.slice(0, 5).map((x) => `\u2022 \\`${x.f}\\``).join('\\n') + (files.length > 5 ? `\\n\u2022 _+${files.length - 5} more_` : '');\n}\n\nasync function revenueSnippet(): Promise<string> {\n  if (!sbHeaders) return '_(revenue query unavailable)_';\n  const since = new Date(Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();\n  try {\n    const url = `${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/pipeline_runs?select=revenue_cents&created_at=gte.${encodeURIComponent(since)}`;\n    const res = await fetch(url, { headers: sbHeaders });\n    if (!res.ok) return '_(pipeline_runs fetch failed)_';\n    const rows = (await res.json()) as Array<{ revenue_cents: number | null }>;\n    if (rows.length === 0) return '_no pipeline runs_';\n    const total = rows.reduce((sum, r) => sum + (r.revenue_cents ?? 0), 0);\n    return `${rows.length} runs \u00b7 R$ ${(total / 100).toFixed(2)} total revenue`;\n  } catch {\n    return '_(revenue query error \u2014 table may not exist yet)_';\n  }\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Compose \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\nasync function compose(): Promise<string> {\n  const date = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);\n  const [git, mesh, revenue] = await Promise.all([gitDelta(), meshGrowth(), revenueSnippet()]);\n  const memory = memoryPromotions();\n  const bok = bokDebts();\n  const incidents = recentIncidents();\n\n  return `\ud83c\udf05 *Morning Briefing \u2014 ${date}*\n\n*Git delta (24h):*\n${git}\n\n*Knowledge Mesh growth:* ${mesh}\n\n*Agent memory promotions:*\n${memory}\n\n*BoK debts:*\n${bok}\n\n*Incidents (24h):*\n${incidents}\n\n*Revenue snippet:* ${revenue}\n\n_Guardian out._`;\n}\n\nconst out = await compose();\nconsole.log(out);\n"
}
```

> 40	> ⚠️ **SCOPE NOTE:** This phase scans changes that exist NOW (before Phase 5 writes HANDOFF.md).
> 41	> A second mandatory scan runs in Phase 5b, after HANDOFF.md is written and before it is committed.
> 42	
> 43	```bash
> 44	# 1a. Secret leak scan — check staged + working tree for hardcoded credentials
> 45	git diff HEAD | grep -E "(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{20,}|STRIPE_|SECRET_KEY|ACCESS_TOKEN|api_key\s*=\s*['\"][^'\"]{10,})" \
> 46	  | grep -v "example\|placeholder\|<.*>\|your-key\|YOUR_" | head -20
> 47	
> 48	# 1b. Edge function JWT enforcement — every user-facing function must verify JWT
> 49	grep -rL "Authorization\|jwt\|JWT\|service_role" supabase/functions/*/index.ts \
> 50	  | grep -v "get-infra-status\|watchdog" || echo "ALL FUNCTIONS: JWT enforced ✅"
> 51	
> 52	# 1c. Client-side coin deduction guard — must NEVER update mco_balance directly from client
> 53	grep -rn "mco_balance.*update\|UPDATE.*mco_balance" src/ \
> 54	  | grep -v "settings\|top.up\|topup\|SettingsPage" | head -10 || echo "NO VIOLATIONS ✅"
> 55	
> 56	# 1d. RLS bypass risk — check for supabase.rpc calls without auth context
> 57	grep -rn "service_role\|bypass.*rls\|rls.*bypass" src/ | head -10 || echo "NO VIOLATIONS ✅"
> 58	
> 59	# 1e. Sensitive data in console.log
> 60	grep -rn "console\.log.*token\|console\.log.*key\|console\.log.*secret\|console\.log.*password" src/ \
> 61	  | head -10 || echo "NO SENSITIVE LOGS ✅"
> 62	```
> 63	
> 64	**Security verdict:** list each check as ✅ PASS / ⚠️ WARN / 🔴 BLOCK.
> 65	Only proceed if no 🔴 findings.
> 66	
> 67	---
> 68	
> 69	### DOCUMENTATION CREDENTIAL RULE (invariant — never violate)
> 70	
> 71	**When writing HANDOFF.md, README.md, or any committed documentation:**
> 72	
> 73	- ✅ DO: describe WHERE a credential is stored → `VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY` is set in `.env` (local) and `GEMINI_API_KEY` in the Supabase vault
> 74	- ✅ DO: reference digest/fingerprint if useful → `GEMINI_API_KEY (digest 0fe0e159...)`
> 75	- 🔴 NEVER: include the actual credential value → `AIzaSyBv...`, `sk-...`, `eyJ...`
> 76	
> 77	> **Rationale:** Phase 1 scans changes that predate the HANDOFF.md write. Any credential embedded
> 78	> in HANDOFF.md during Phase 5 escapes Phase 1 entirely and goes directly to the remote.
> 79	> This rule is the primary prevention layer; Phase 5b is the detection backstop.
> 80	
> 81	---
> 82	
> 83	## PHASE 2 — GRANULAR COMMITS
> 84	
> 85	Group uncommitted changes by concern and commit each group separately.
> 86	
> 87	**Commit rules (from memory + project convention):**
> 88	- Prefix: `feat:`, `fix:`, `refactor:`, `chore:`, `docs:`
> 89	- Body: one paragraph explaining the WHY (milestone orientation, not diff description)
> 90	- **NEVER** add `Co-Authored-By` trailer
> 91	- **NEVER** use `git add -A` — stage files explicitly by name
> 92	- Skip: `build_log.txt`, `node_modules/`, `dist/`, `.env`, `check_types.ts`, `scratch/`
> 93	
> 94	Stage and commit each logical group before moving to the next phase.

> # OpenClaw v6.3.1 — Neural Synchronization & Agent Army
> 
> ## Contexto
> 
> Sprint v6.3.0 (Canvas 4.2a) está selada. OpenClaw bootstrap entregou squad de 4 agentes (main/artisan/engineer/scientist) com SOUL.md operacional, Telegram bot `@claw_gcrux` (token 8668689828) wired no `~/.openclaw/openclaw.json`, e cron job nativo `Memory Dreaming Promotion` (03:00 diário) já registrado em `~/.openclaw/cron/jobs.json` — porém todos os SQLite de memória estão **vazios** (`chunks=0, files=0`). O motor de "Dreaming" existe mas não tem combustível: a Knowledge Mesh tem 7466+ nós embedados em Supabase mas NÃO há ponte para os SQLite locais que o indexer OpenClaw consome.
> 
> Esta sprint:
> 1. **Constrói a ponte Supabase→disco→indexer OpenClaw** (Two-tier hybrid: rich/AST).
> 2. **Escala o squad para 6 agentes** com Departmental Army (4 Heads L1 + main synthesizer + guardian dreaming-watcher) usando overlay shared + per-agent.
> 3. **Sela o loop Nightly Dreaming completo** (bridge refresh 02:30 → dream nativo 03:00 → morning briefing Telegram 03:30).
> 
> **BoK Gate:** OpenClaw segue com BoK 0/9 (override consciente registrado em v6.2.0, SLA 7d em curso, scientist agent = owner). Esta sprint **expande** o escopo OpenClaw — recomendado disparar `/bok-scribe openclaw` como follow-up imediato após a seal (não bloqueia esta execução, mas zera a janela do SLA).
>

> ## Phase 1 — Neural Bridge (Two-tier Hybrid)
> 
> ### 1.1 Infraestrutura física
> 
> ```bash
> # Workspace bridge output (master corpus)
> mkdir -p /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/memory/mcorch-export/{tier1,tier2,_shared,_overlays,_meta}
> 
> # Per-agent memory input dirs (consumidos pelo indexer)
> mkdir -p /home/ubuntu/.openclaw/agents/{main,artisan,engineer,scientist,marketing-growth,guardian}/memory
> ```
> 
> Adicionar entrada em `.gitignore` para `memory/mcorch-export/` (corpus regenerável; não versionar 8k+ arquivos).
> 
> ### 1.2 Bridge script — `scripts/bridge-mesh-to-openclaw.ts`
> 
> **Mirror direto do padrão de** `scripts/backfill-embeddings.ts:1-63` (loader `.env` próprio + REST pagination com Range headers + auth via `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`).
> 
> **Schema-source:** `mcorch_nodes` (id, node_type, name, content, metadata jsonb, stability_score, revenue_impact, user_id, project_id, created_at, last_seen_at) + `mcorch_edges` (source_id, target_id, relation_type, weight, metadata).
> 
> **Two-tier classification** (por `node_type`):
> 
> | Tier | node_types | Output | Conteúdo |
> |------|-----------|--------|----------|
> | **Tier 1 (Strategy)** | `decision`, `milestone`, `architecture`, `requirement`, `handoff`, `observation`, `documentation_suite`, `system`, `conversation` | `memory/mcorch-export/tier1/<type>/<id>.md` | YAML frontmatter completo + `content` body integral |
> | **Tier 2 (Symbols)** | `file`, `function`, `arrow_function`, `interface` (AST) | `memory/mcorch-export/tier2/<type>/<id>.md` | YAML frontmatter + apenas `path` + `signature` (de `metadata`) + `summary` (primeiros 200 chars de `content`) |
> 
> **Edges:** `memory/mcorch-export/_meta/edges.jsonl` (uma aresta por linha).
> 
> **Manifest:** `memory/mcorch-export/_meta/manifest.json` com `{ exported_at, tier1_count, tier2_count, edge_count, source_total }` para auditoria.
> 
> **Pagination:** loop com `pageSize=1000`, `Range: ${from}-${to}` + `Range-Unit: items` (idêntico ao backfill).
> 
> **Skip embeddings:** OpenClaw re-embeda localmente; descartar coluna `embedding` do select.
> 
> **Filtros adicionais:** Tier 2 só exporta `metadata->>language IS NOT NULL` (skipa AST mal-formado); ambos pulam `stability_score < 0.05` (FSRS-decayed esquecidos).
> 
> ### 1.3 Overlay distribution — `scripts/distribute-mesh-to-agents.ts`
> 
> Após o bridge produzir o master corpus, popula per-agent input dirs com **shared + agent-specific overlay** (resposta confirmada do Sovereign).
> 
> **Shared base** (symlink → todos os 6 agentes):
> - `tier1/decision/`, `tier1/milestone/`, `tier1/system/`, `tier1/handoff/`
> 
> **Overlays específicos** (symlink → agente apropriado):
> 
> | Agente | Overlay |
> |--------|---------|
> | `engineer` | + `tier1/architecture/` + `tier1/observation/` + **todo `tier2/`** |
> | `scientist` | + `tier1/documentation_suite/` + `tier1/requirement/` + `tier1/conversation/` + `tier1/observation/` |
> | `artisan` | + `tier1/conversation/` + filtro `metadata->tags ?? '' LIKE '%ui%' OR '%ux%'` em `architecture` |
> | `marketing-growth` | + filtro `revenue_impact > 0` em qualquer tier1 + filtro `metadata->tags LIKE '%affiliate%' OR '%apollo%' OR '%gcrux%'` |
> | `main` | + **tudo de tier1** (synthesizer view) |
> | `guardian` | + `tier1/handoff/` + `tier1/milestone/` + `tier1/observation/` (Dreaming logs view) |
> 
> Implementação: symlinks relativos (`ln -sf`) para evitar duplicação física; cada agente vê só o que importa via `~/.openclaw/agents/<id>/memory/<symlink>`.
>

> - **Core Capabilities:** (1) Daily git delta parsing (`git log --since="24 hours ago"`) (2) MEMORY.md aggregation across agents (3) Telegram briefing composition + delivery (4) Self-optimization detection (proposes CLAUDE.md edits via PR-style diff em `.claude/context/proposed-claude-md-<date>.md` para aprovação do Sovereign — NUNCA edita CLAUDE.md direto) (5) Morning briefing posting via Telegram bot
> - **Decision Scope:** ✅ decide o que entra no briefing matinal · ✅ propõe edits a CLAUDE.md · ❌ NÃO commita edits a CLAUDE.md sem aprovação · ❌ NÃO escala incident (esse caminho é do Engineer)
> - **Inputs:** `~/.openclaw/agents/*/MEMORY.md` (deltas) · `git log` (24h window) · `mcorch_nodes` inserts do dia (filtro por `created_at > now() - interval '24h'`)
> - **Outputs:** `/var/log/mcorch-nightly-dream.log` · Telegram message para `@claw_gcrux` · proposed CLAUDE.md diff (quando aplicável)
> - **Collaboration Map:** Scientist ← BoK debt reminders · Engineer ← infra anomalies do dia · Marketing-Growth ← revenue digest · Sovereign ← morning briefing
>

> 
> ## Phase 3 — Nightly Dreaming Alignment
> 
> ### 3.1 Cron pipeline (3 jobs encadeados)
> 
> | Hora | Script | Função |
> |------|--------|--------|
> | `30 2 * * *` | `scripts/nightly-bridge-refresh.sh` | Re-roda `bridge-mesh-to-openclaw.ts` + `distribute-mesh-to-agents.ts` (captura nós novos das últimas 24h) + `pnpm openclaw memory index --agent=<each>` |
> | `0 3 * * *` | (existente) `Memory Dreaming Promotion` em `~/.openclaw/cron/jobs.json` | OpenClaw nativo: promove weighted recalls para `MEMORY.md` por agente |
> | `30 3 * * *` | `scripts/morning-briefing.sh` | Guardian compõe briefing (git log delta + MEMORY.md deltas + open BoK debts + revenue snippet) e POSTA via `https://api.telegram.org/bot<TOKEN>/sendMessage` para `@claw_gcrux` |
> 
> ### 3.2 `scripts/nightly-bridge-refresh.sh`
> 
> ```bash
> #!/usr/bin/env bash
> set -euo pipefail
> cd /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra
> LOG=/var/log/mcorch-nightly-bridge.log
> { date; bun run scripts/bridge-mesh-to-openclaw.ts --since="24 hours ago";
>   bun run scripts/distribute-mesh-to-agents.ts;
>   for a in main artisan engineer scientist marketing-growth guardian; do
>     pnpm openclaw memory index --agent="$a" || echo "FAIL $a"
>   done;
>   date; } >> "$LOG" 2>&1
> ```
> 
> Failure path: ao detectar fail em qualquer step, escreve incident JSON em `/tmp/openclaw-incidents/nightly-bridge-<ts>.json` e POST best-effort ao gateway `http://127.0.0.1:18789/agents/engineer/incidents` (mirror do padrão `scripts/watchdog-mcorch.sh:69-131`).
> 
> ### 3.3 `scripts/morning-briefing.sh`
> 
> ```bash
> #!/usr/bin/env bash
> set -euo pipefail
> cd /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra
> LOG=/var/log/mcorch-nightly-dream.log
> TOKEN=$(jq -r '.channels.telegram.botToken' /home/ubuntu/.openclaw/openclaw.json)
> CHAT="@claw_gcrux"
> 
> # Compose briefing (markdown)
> BRIEF=$(bun run scripts/compose-morning-briefing.ts)  # outputs markdown to stdout
> 
> # Post to Telegram
> curl -s -X POST "https://api.telegram.org/bot${TOKEN}/sendMessage" \
>   -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
>   -d "$(jq -n --arg c "$CHAT" --arg t "$BRIEF" \
>     '{chat_id:$c, text:$t, parse_mode:"Markdown", disable_web_page_preview:true}')" \
>   >> "$LOG" 2>&1
> ```
> 
> `scripts/compose-morning-briefing.ts` (novo): TS script que lê git log 24h, conta nós novos no mesh (Supabase query), agrega MEMORY.md deltas dos 6 agentes, lista BoK debts abertas (re-roda o check de `/handson` v3 PRE-FLIGHT), lista incidents do dia em `/tmp/openclaw-incidents/`, formata como markdown enxuto (≤ 4096 chars per Telegram limit).
> 
> ### 3.4 Crontab append
> 
> ```cron
> 30 2 * * *  /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/nightly-bridge-refresh.sh
> 30 3 * * *  /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/morning-briefing.sh
> ```
> 
> (Existing crons preservados: watchdog, auto-publish, audit-4cs, ast-ingest, dreaming-promotion.)
>

> ## Verification
> 
> End-to-end (dry-run primeiro, real depois):
> 
> 1. **Bridge dry-run:** `bun run scripts/bridge-mesh-to-openclaw.ts --dry-run` — confirma counts (Tier 1 ~5500, Tier 2 ~1750, edges ~3500). Não escreve.
> 2. **Bridge real:** rodar sem `--dry-run`. Validar `ls memory/mcorch-export/tier1/decision/ | wc -l` ≈ contagem esperada de decisions no mesh.
> 3. **Distribute:** `bun run scripts/distribute-mesh-to-agents.ts` — validar `ls -la /home/ubuntu/.openclaw/agents/engineer/memory/` mostra symlinks para shared base + tier2 + architecture/observation.
> 4. **Index:** `pnpm openclaw memory index --agent=scientist` — validar exit 0 + `pnpm openclaw memory status` reporta `chunks > 1500` para scientist.
> 5. **Repeat indexing for all 6 agents** — log success por agente.
> 6. **Manual dream trigger:** `pnpm openclaw memory promote --agent=engineer` (ou equivalente do CLI) — validar `~/.openclaw/agents/engineer/MEMORY.md` ganha entradas novas.
> 7. **Manual morning briefing:** `bash scripts/morning-briefing.sh` — validar mensagem chega em `@claw_gcrux` no Telegram + `/var/log/mcorch-nightly-dream.log` ganha entry de sucesso.
> 8. **Cron sanity:** `crontab -l` mostra as 2 linhas novas. Aguardar 24h para validar execução autônoma do ciclo completo.
> 9. **Failure path:** simular falha no Supabase (env var errada temporariamente) + rodar bridge — validar incident JSON em `/tmp/openclaw-incidents/` + POST tentado ao gateway.
> 10. **TS check:** `npx tsc --noEmit` — zero erros após adicionar os scripts novos.
> 11. **BoK Gate follow-up:** disparar `/bok-scribe openclaw` em sessão dedicada (não bloqueia esta seal, mas zera o SLA 7d do override consciente).
